### [97] [A Modified Dependence Measure Related to Chatterjee's Rank Correlation: Theoretical Properties and Asymptotic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07844)
*Chuancun Yin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对Chatterjee秩相关系数ξ的改进度量方法，推导了其等价表示形式，构建了估计量并证明了强一致性和渐近分布性质，同时给出了自然扩展框架。蒙特卡洛模拟表明，所提估计量在有限样本下优于Chatterjee的秩相关，尤其在控制第一类错误方面表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chatterjee提出的秩相关系数ξ虽具有优良性质（ξ=0当且仅当独立，ξ=1当且仅当Y是X的可测函数），但现有估计量在有限样本下的表现仍有改进空间，特别是在控制第一类错误方面存在不足。

Method: 从理论上推导ξ(X,Y)的多个等价表示形式，基于此构建一个新的估计量，并建立其强一致性与渐近分布理论，同时提出框架的自然扩展。通过蒙特卡洛模拟与Chatterjee的秩相关进行比较验证。

Result: 推导出了改进度量ξ的多重等价表示；所构建的估计量具备强一致性和已知的渐近分布；模拟实验表明新估计量在有限样本下整体优于Chatterjee的秩相关系数，尤其在控制第一类错误（Type I error）方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 该改进度量在保持Chatterjee系数理论优势（不依赖单调性或线性）的同时，在有限样本推断方面具有更优的统计性能，是对现有依赖度量框架的有效补充与提升。

Abstract: In his recent breakthrough work [JASA, 2021], Chatterjee proposed a rank-based correlation coefficient $ξ(X,Y)$ to measure the dependence of a random variable $Y$ on $X$. Unlike classical measures such as Pearson, Spearman, or Kendall, $ξ$ satisfies $ξ=0$ if and only if $X$ and $Y$ are independent, and $ξ=1$ if and only if $Y$ is a measurable function of $X$, without requiring monotonicity or linearity. This paper proposes a refined measure of $ξ(X,Y)$ and investigates its theoretical properties. We derive several equivalent representations, construct an estimator, and establish its strong consistency as well as its asymptotic distribution. A natural extension of the proposed framework is also presented. The Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed estimator outperforms Chatterjee's rank correlation in terms of finite-sample performance, particularly in controlling Type I error under the null.

</details>


### [98] [Uniform Testability Implies Asymmetric Testability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07854)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从均匀固定时间检验到全样本单侧错误控制与均匀未来尾部控制的过滤级转换方法，并以此证明了Ryabko猜想5.2中的第一个蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定时间检验的误差控制通常只能保证在预先设定的时间点有效，但实际应用中需要在所有样本量下控制Type I错误，并在每个更新时刻之后对未来错误提供均匀保证。本文旨在弥合固定时间检验与全样本/未来尾部控制之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 给定一个自适应确定性二值检验（其最坏情况固定时间误差趋于零），构造中选择具有预定可求和错误预算的更新时刻，并将每个选定决策保持到下一次更新。通过在每个假设类别上以剩余预算均匀约束后续错误概率，实现从固定时间检验到全样本与未来尾部控制的转换。

Result: 对于每个预设显著性水平，构造出的检验能在每个样本量下控制Type I错误；从每个更新时刻起，任何后续错误的概率在剩余预算下均匀有界。检验在两种假设下都几乎必然一致收敛并最终正确。将该转换应用于有限字母坐标过程，证明了Ryabko猜想5.2中的第一个蕴含关系。

Conclusion: 该转换框架提供了一种系统性的方法论，将固定时间检验的强大理论成果推广到在线/序贯场景中，实现了对错误的全样本期与未来尾部统一控制，并为解决Ryabko猜想提供了关键的理论工具。

Abstract: We prove a filtration-level conversion from uniform fixed-time testing to all-sample one-sided error control and uniform future-tail control. Given an adapted deterministic binary test whose worst-case fixed-time error tends to zero, the construction selects update times with prescribed summable error budgets and holds each selected decision until the next update. For every prescribed significance level, the construction yields a test that controls Type I error at every sample size; from each update time onward, the probability of any subsequent error is bounded uniformly over each hypothesis class by the remaining budget. Hence the test is uniformly consistent and eventually correct almost surely under both hypotheses. Applying the conversion to finite-alphabet coordinate processes proves the first implication in Ryabko's Conjecture 5.2.

</details>


### [99] [Independence Is Not Always Consistently Testable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07860)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了联合平稳遍历二元过程中坐标过程间的独立性检验问题，证明了不存在逐点一致（pointwise consistent）的检验方法：任何对每个相关律统计功效趋于1的检验程序，必然在某些独立的平稳遍历律上存在不消失的假阳性概率。定量地，存在某个坐标独立的联合平稳遍历律，其假阳性概率的上极限至少为1/2。这表明即使在平稳性和遍历性假设下，独立性也无法从不断增长的有穷样本中被一致判定。证明结合了对角线构造与稀有标记技术，在选定尺度上制造可检测的相关性，同时收敛到坐标独立的极限过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立性检验是统计推断的基本问题，但在非参数（无模型假设）场景下，现有的不可能性结果主要针对不平稳或不遍历的过程。本文的动机在于回答一个更基本的问题：即使过程满足平稳性和遍历性这两个在时间序列分析中最常用的结构性假设，独立性检验是否可能做到逐点一致？

Method: 采用构造性反证法。通过"对角线构造"精心设计一系列联合平稳遍历的二元过程，其中融入"稀有标记"（rare markers），这些标记只在选定的特定尺度上产生可检测的坐标相关性，同时随着样本量增大，这些构造过程收敛到一个坐标独立的极限平稳遍历过程。这样的构造迫使任何"一致检验"在无穷多个样本量上对某些独立过程产生非零假阳性。

Result: 证明了不存在逐点一致（in probability）的独立性检验。具体地，若一个检验程序对每个相关律的幂趋于1，则存在某个坐标独立的联合平稳遍历二元过程，使得假阳性概率的上极限至少为1/2。这一结果从不可能性角度严格限制了这一问题的可检验性。

Conclusion: 即使在平稳性和遍历性这一对表征"无时间趋势、状态充分混合"的理想条件下，二元过程坐标独立性也本质上是不可一致检验的。这一负面结果表明，对于该问题需要更强的假设（如特定参数模型、混合速率约束或有限阶马尔可夫性）才能建立可行的检验程序。

Abstract: We study the problem of testing independence between the coordinate processes of a jointly stationary ergodic binary process from finite observations. We prove that no test is pointwise consistent in probability: any procedure whose power tends to one against every dependent law must have nonvanishing false-positive probability on some independent stationary ergodic law along infinitely many sample sizes. Quantitatively, for some jointly stationary ergodic law with independent coordinate processes, the false-positive probability has limsup at least $1/2$. Thus, even under stationarity and ergodicity, independence cannot be consistently decided from increasingly long finite samples. The proof combines a diagonal construction with rare markers that create detectable dependence at selected scales while converging to a limiting process whose coordinates are independent.

</details>


### [100] [On the optimality of antithetic randomization for cross-validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08089)
*Srijan Chattopadhyay,Sifan Liu,Snigdha Panigrahi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究经典正态均值问题中交叉验证估计器的随机化方案设计，证明了对光滑估计器，相关系数为ρ=-1/(K-1)的对偶随机化是使随机化可约方差在偏差消失时保持有界的充要条件；构建了一类对偶方案，其中联合正态方案在极小极大意义下最优；对非光滑估计器，对偶随机化可改善渐近率，而控制变量法可在已知间断点时恢复有界方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典正态均值问题中，通过正态随机化扰动数据可构造独立的训练-测试折，但跨验证估计器的偏差取决于随机化变量的边际分布，方差取决于联合分布。这自然引出两个核心问题：哪种联合分布最优？如何构造相应的随机化方案？

Method: 本文通过理论分析证明：(i)对于光滑估计器，证明对偶随机化（成对相关ρ=-1/(K-1)）是方差有界的充要条件；(ii)给出一般性构造方法，产生一类对偶方案，并在其中证明联合正态方案是极小极大最优的；(iii)针对具有有限跳跃间断点的非光滑估计器，分析对偶随机化对渐近率的影响，并引入控制变量法处理已知间断点情形。

Result: (i)对光滑估计器，对偶随机化ρ=-1/(K-1)是随机化可约方差有界的充要条件；(ii)构建了一类对偶方案，联合正态方案在其中为极小极大最优；(iii)对非光滑估计器，对偶随机化改善了可约方差的渐近率，已知间断点时控制变量法可恢复有界方差。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了正态均值问题中随机化方案的联合分布最优选择问题，确定了最优对偶相关结构，展示了联合正态方案的最优性，并为非光滑估计器提供了实用的方差控制策略，为交叉验证中随机化方案的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In the classical normal means problem, independent train--test folds can be constructed by perturbing the data with normal randomization. Averaging over $K$ such folds yields a cross-validation estimator whose bias depends on the marginal distribution of the randomization variables, while its variance depends on their joint distribution. This raises the questions: which joint law is optimal, and how to construct the corresponding randomization scheme? We show that: (i) for smooth estimators, antithetic randomization with pairwise correlation $ρ=-1/(K-1)$ is necessary and sufficient for the reducible variance due to randomization to remain bounded as the bias vanishes; (ii) a general construction yields a class of antithetic schemes, within which the jointly normal scheme is minimax optimal; and (iii) for non-smooth estimators with finitely many jump discontinuities, antithetic randomization improves the asymptotic rate of the reducible variance, while a simple control variate restores bounded variance when the discontinuities are known.

</details>


### [101] [Complete Monotonicity of the Srivastava-Tomovski Function and Its Laplace-Wright Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08198)
*Rey R. Cuenca,Welfredo R. Patungan,Ruzzel Ragas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了负轴Srivastava-Tomovski函数的完全单调性，将其广义Wright级数与正轴Laplace-Wright实现分离。级数仅在Δ=1+α-κ>0时为整函数，此时当α≤κ且κβ≥αγ时完全单调。对于0<α<κ，Laplace-Wright实现可延伸至有限半径和零半径相位，其归一化内部测度是Wang的幂偏置Wright定律在t→t^κ下的前推，所有可容许测度的质量为1/Γ(β)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在系统刻画负轴Srivastava-Tomovski函数的完全单调性条件，建立其与Laplace-Wright实现、Wright型概率定律之间的深层联系，并统一已知的Prabhakar充分性和负值结果。

Method: 采用级数整性分析（Δ=1+α-κ>0条件判别）、完全单调性的参数域刻画（α≤κ且κβ≥αγ）、通过有限符号Laplace唯一性和矩支撑论证处理排除区域，并将Wright密度转化为先验测度的前推表示。

Result: 获得了完全单调性的精确刻画条件；建立了归一化内部测度与Wang的幂偏置Wright定律之间的等价关系；证明了每个可容许测度的质量为1/Γ(β)；通过κ=1的特例统一了已知的Prabhakar充分性和负值结果，并揭示了先验支撑坍缩机制。

Conclusion: 该研究为Srivastava-Tomovski函数的分析性质提供了完整的理论框架，建立了特殊函数分析与概率测度理论之间的桥梁，并统一了该领域已有的部分结果。

Abstract: For positive parameters, we study complete monotonicity of the negative-axis Srivastava-Tomovski function while keeping its generalized-Wright series separate from a positive-axis Laplace-Wright realization. The series is entire only when $Δ=1+α-κ>0$; in that regime it is completely monotone precisely when $α\leqκ$ and $κβ\geqαγ$. For $0<α<κ$, the Laplace-Wright realization extends through the finite-radius and zero-radius phases and obeys the same characterization. Its normalized interior measure is a prior law: with $a=α/κ$, $μ=β-aγ$, it is the pushforward under $t\mapsto t^κ$ of Wang's power-biased Wright law, based on the Ferreira-Simon Wright density. Their results supply the interior positivity, admissible parameter region, moments, and beta/atomic endpoints. We connect these laws to the analytic phases of the four-parameter realization and use finite signed Laplace uniqueness and moment-support arguments for the excluded regions. The specialization $κ=1$ consolidates known Prabhakar sufficiency and negative-value results with the prior support-collapse mechanism. Every admissible measure has mass $1/Γ(β)$.

</details>


### [102] [On inferential equivalence classes of causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08327)
*Charlotte Edgar,Heather Battey*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究因果模型的置信集构建问题。由于同一马可夫等价类中的因果模型在统计上不可区分，论文利用Cox和Wermuth（1993）的无约束参数化方法构建基于标准似然理论的因果模型置信集，并通过Evans（2020）的几何框架分析各模型被纳入置信集的概率行为，包括在可检测性边界上的稳定、缓慢衰减和快速衰减三种情况，同时探讨混合模型场景下的包含概率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果模型在统计推断中面临根本性的可识别性问题：同一马可夫等价类中的模型在任何样本量下都无法区分，而小样本下还可能有多个等价类与数据兼容。因此，需要以置信集的形式呈现因果推断证据，但目前对置信集中各模型被纳入概率的结构性特征缺乏深入理解。

Method: 利用Cox和Wermuth（1993）提出的无约束参数化方法来表示逆协方差矩阵，避免锥约束带来的分布近似困难，从而基于标准似然理论构建因果模型置信集；采用Evans（2020）的几何公式来分析置信集的包含概率特性，尤其在真实参数处于可检测性边界时的行为。

Result: 研究发现，在真实参数处于可检测性边界时，不同因果模型被纳入置信集的概率呈现三种模式：稳定于标称水平、随样本量缓慢衰减、随样本量快速衰减。在混合因果模型的场景中，混合权重和各模型相对于候选模型的几何关系共同影响包含概率。

Conclusion: 论文从真实因果机制的结构属性出发，系统探究了因果推断中可达到的极限，为理解置信集方法在因果推断中的能力和局限提供了理论基础，揭示了样本量、模型几何结构与包含概率之间的深层关系。

Abstract: Causal models in the same Markov equivalence class are, in the absence of strong assumptions, statistically indistinguishable at any sample size. For modest sample sizes there is also the possibility that several such classes are compatible with the data, pointing to a confidence set of causal models as the appropriate presentation of evidence. Non-identifiability of Gaussian causal models from the same Markov equivalence class corresponds to a plurality of inverse-covariance representations in the unconstrained parametrisation of Cox and Wermuth (1993). This parametrisation, avoiding conic constraints that would otherwise complicate distributional approximations, facilitates construction of, and theoretical analysis for, a confidence set of causal models based on standard likelihood theory. By drawing on a geometric formulation of Evans (2020), we provide insight into which causal models are most likely to be included in the confidence set when the true parameter values of the generating model are at the borderline of detectability, delineating those models whose inclusion probabilities are stable at the nominal level, slowly decaying with sample size, and quickly decaying with sample size. We also study settings in which two or more causal models are in operation, exploring how the mixture weights, and the geometry of the models in the mixture relative to candidate models, interact with the inclusion probabilities. The purpose of the paper is to probe, from the perspective of structural properties of the true causal mechanism, the limits of what is achievable in causal inferential settings.

</details>


### [103] [Nonparametric quantile inference using Dirichlet processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08355)
*Nils Lid Hjort,Sonia Petrone*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角出发，基于Dirichlet过程对分位数进行非参数推断，刻画了分位数的后验分布，并给出了后验均值与方差的显式表达式。与分布函数的贝叶斯估计不同，分位数函数的贝叶斯估计是光滑曲线。文中还给出了Bernstein-von Mises型定理，展示了分位数过程的极限后验分布，并与核平滑分位数估计建立了联系。作为副产品，作者还开发了一个无需平滑参数的非参数密度估计器，其支撑与数据范围完全一致。此外，还提供了分位数相关量的非参数贝叶斯估计，包括Lorenz曲线、Gini指数、Doksum位移曲线、Parzen比较分布（两样本情形）以及含协变量的分位数回归函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分位数推断在统计学中具有重要价值，但基于Dirichlet过程的非参数贝叶斯分位数推断尚未得到充分研究。现有方法中，分布函数的贝叶斯估计导数不光滑，难以直接获得好的分位数估计，因此需要一种既能保持贝叶斯框架优势又能产生光滑分位数曲线估计的方法。

Method: 利用Dirichlet过程作为先验，构建分位数的后验分布特征；推导后验均值和方差的显式公式；通过Bernstein-von Mises型定理刻画分位数过程的极限后验分布；将贝叶斯分位数估计与核平滑估计建立理论联系；并扩展至密度估计及其他分位数相关量（如Lorenz曲线、Gini指数、Doksum位移曲线、Parzen比较分布和分位数回归）的贝叶斯非参数推断。

Result: 获得了分位数后验分布的完整刻画，证明了分位数函数贝叶斯估计具有光滑性（优于分布函数估计），并建立了Bernstein-von Mises型极限定理。同时给出了一个无需平滑参数的自适应非参数密度估计器，以及多个分位数相关量的非参数贝叶斯估计。

Conclusion: 基于Dirichlet过程的贝叶斯方法为分位数及其相关量提供了一种统一的非参数推断框架，所提出的估计量具有光滑性、理论保证（后验极限定理）和实际可用性（无平滑参数依赖），丰富了非参数贝叶斯分位数推断的理论与应用。

Abstract: This chapter deals with nonparametric inference for quantiles from a Bayesian perspective, using the Dirichlet process. The posterior distribution for quantiles is characterised, enabling also explicit formulae for posterior mean and variance. Unlike the Bayes estimator for the distribution function, our Bayes estimator for the quantile function is a smooth curve. A Bernstein--von Mises type theorem is given, exhibiting the limiting posterior distribution of the quantile process. Links to kernel-smoothed quantile estimators are provided. As a side product we develop an automatic nonparametric density estimator, free of smoothing parameters, with support exactly matching that of the data range. Nonparametric Bayes estimators are also provided for other quantile-related quantities, including the Lorenz curve and the Gini index, for Doksum's shift curve and for Parzen's comparison distribution in two-sample situations, and finally for the quantile regression function in situations with covariates.

</details>


### [104] [Pointwise convergence of purely random partition estimators: from random trees to prototype rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08360)
*Jérémy Bettinger,François Portier,Adrien Saumard*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯随机划分估计器在非参数回归中的逐点收敛速度，提出了"形状正则性"这一几何判据作为达到极小极大最优率的充要条件（对数因子以内），并据此解释了中心树、均匀树、Mondrian树及Voronoi划分（Proto-NN、OptiNet）等方法的收敛性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究对不同类型的随机划分估计器（纯随机树、原型规则等）给出了不一致的收敛率结论，缺乏统一的几何框架来解释为何某些划分方法需要超对数修正项，而另一些能达到极小极大最优率；同时Proto-NN的逐点浓度界是一个悬而未决的开放问题。

Method: 引入"形状正则性"这一单一几何判据，将单元直径与体积相关联；分别检验中心树、均匀树、Mondrian树以及Voronoi划分中的Proto-NN和OptiNet是否满足该判据，并基于此推导逐点收敛率和浓度界。

Result: 中心树和均匀树的单元纵横比随分裂次数呈指数增长（概率有正下界），因而不满足形状正则性，导致误差界出现超对数修正；Mondrian树满足概率意义下的形状正则性，达到极小极大率。对Proto-NN首次给出逐点浓度界，解决了Györfi和Weiss的开放问题；OptiNet凭借η-网构造以显著更优的成功概率达到极小极大率，在适当参数下甚至几乎必然收敛。

Conclusion: 形状正则性是统一理解和比较各类随机划分估计器性能的关键几何条件，它同时解释了已有方法的局限性并为设计更优估计器提供了理论指引。

Abstract: We study pointwise convergence rates of purely random partition estimators in nonparametric regression, where the partition -- into hyper-rectangles by purely random trees, or into Voronoi cells by prototype rules -- is built independently of the responses. Our analysis rests on a single geometric criterion, shape regularity, relating the diameter of a cell to its volume, which is shown by Bettinger, Portier and Saumard (2026) to be necessary and sufficient, up to logarithmic factors, for achieving the minimax rate $n^{-1/(d+2)}$. We show that centered and uniform trees are not shape-regular -- their cells' aspect ratio grows exponentially with the number of splits with probability bounded away from zero -- explaining the super-logarithmic corrections in their error bounds, whereas Mondrian trees, whose splits adapt to the current cell geometry, are shape-regular in probability and attain the minimax rate. The same analysis applied to Voronoi partitions yields the first pointwise concentration bounds for Proto-NN, resolving an open problem of Györfi and Weiss (2021), and shows that OptiNet achieves the minimax rate with markedly better success probability -- even almost surely, for a suitable choice of parameters -- thanks to its $η$-net construction.

</details>


### [105] [Lasso Universality Under Linearly Dependent Covariates in the Sparse Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08390)
*Soroush Mesforush,Rahul Parhi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了在稀疏回归（Lasso）中，当协变量矩阵的行和列同时具有线性依赖（而非独立同分布）时，高斯普适性依然成立，并通过数值实验验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯普适性研究大多假设协变量矩阵为独立同分布或仅考虑块依赖、特定行列依赖等特殊形式，尚缺乏对行和列同时混合依赖情况的系统研究，因此本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对稀疏回归中的Lasso问题，在非高斯协变量具有行和列线性依赖的设定下，证明高斯普适性定理，并通过多种稀疏轮廓的数值实验进行验证。

Result: 成功证明了Lasso在行和列同时存在线性依赖的非高斯协变量下的高斯普适性，其允许的依赖结构比以往文献更广泛；数值实验支持所提出的普适性结论。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了高斯普适性理论的适用范围，首次系统地处理了行和列同时混合依赖的协变量结构，为高维稀疏估计的普适性研究提供了更一般性的理论基础。

Abstract: Throughout the last decade, Gaussian universality has been widely studied for high-dimensional estimation problems. Most of the literature focuses on i.i.d. sensing matrices or accounts for special forms of dependence, such as block dependence or other specific row/column dependencies. More general simultaneous row and column mixing has not yet been fully studied. In this paper, we focus on that setting. We prove a Gaussian universality theorem for the lasso in the sparse regime, where the non- Gaussian covariates have linearly dependent rows and columns. To the best of our knowledge, our setting permits a broader simultaneous row and column dependence structure than those treated in much of the prior universality literature. Numerical illustrations for various sparse profiles support the universality claims of this paper.

</details>


### [106] [Ultra-high-dimensional spot support screening in continuous-time regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08582)
*Haibin Zhu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究高频连续时间回归中的变量筛选问题，针对时变（spot）回归系数的支撑集进行识别。在坐标截断和有限变差跳跃条件下，建立了局部边际相关向量与协变量相关矩阵估计的一致逐元素界，并推导出与局部化及多重性阶匹配的最小化最大下界。同时提出了一种带有路径化Lepski自适应的部分残差spot迭代筛选方法，保证筛选取舍性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频金融数据中回归系数随时间变化，如何在高维动态设定下可靠地识别对响应变量有显著影响的协变量集合（支撑集）是一个重要且未充分解决的问题。已有的变量筛选方法多针对静态或低维场景，缺乏对高频连续时间回归中时变系数支撑集的系统理论保证。

Method: 1) 在坐标截断和有限变差跳跃假设下，对局部边际相关向量和协变量相关矩阵的局部估计建立统一逐元素上界；2) 在局部平均α-Hölder条件下推导筛选率界；3) 建立列表大小的极小极大下界；4) 提出部分残差spot迭代筛选算法，结合路径化Lepski自适应进行带宽选择，仅需当前活跃集对应的相关列。

Result: 筛选率达到每个log p/n的α/(2α+1)次幂（忽略对数因子），在保持列表大小d满足log(p/d)与log p同阶时，下界与局部化和多重性阶匹配。所提方法在稀疏特征值和标准化beta-min条件下具备确定筛选取舍性质，并通过模拟和高频因子应用验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为高频连续时间回归中的时变支撑集筛选提供了完整的理论框架与实用算法，给出了匹配的上界与下界，并证明了所提迭代筛选方法的可靠性，在高频金融应用中展示了良好的实证表现。

Abstract: We study variable screening for high-frequency continuous-time regression, targeting the support of the spot regression coefficient. We establish uniform entrywise bounds for local estimates of the marginal correlation vector and covariate correlation matrix under coordinatewise truncation and finite-variation jumps. Under a local averaged $α$-Hölder condition, the rate is $\{(\log p)/n\}^{α/(2α+1)}$, up to logarithmic factors. This yields sure screening for the marginal support. We also derive a list-size minimax lower bound that matches the localization and multiplicity order when the retained list size $d$ satisfies $\log(p/d)\asymp\log p$. For regression-support recovery under local dependence, we propose partial-residual spot iterative screening with pathwise Lepski adaptation. Under sparse eigenvalues and standardized beta-min, the procedure has the sure screening property and requires only correlation columns associated with the current active set. Simulation studies and a high-frequency factor application illustrate its performance.

</details>


### [107] [Partial Differential Equation Barriers to Identifiability in Infinite Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08597)
*Dung Le,Nicola Bariletto,Alessandro Rinaldo,Nhat Ho*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限混合模型中混合度量的可辨识性问题，证明在许多常见情况下，不可辨识性可以用核族关于其参数的微分结构来刻画。当核被参数空间上的非平凡微分或差分-微分算子湮灭时，存在无穷多个不同的混合度量产生相同的混合密度。文章给出了可验证的条件，涵盖多种常见分布族，并补充了不可辨识情况下Wasserstein距离估计误差的极小极大下界，同时描述了保持可辨识性的三类核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限混合模型是统计学中的核心工具，但混合度量的可辨识性（即能否从混合密度唯一恢复混合度量）是进行可靠统计推断的前提。现有研究对有限混合模型的可辨识性已有较充分认识，但无限混合模型中的可辨识性条件尚不清楚。作者旨在系统刻画无限混合模型中不可辨识性的本质特征，并给出可操作的条件。

Method: 作者从微分算子角度入手，将不可辨识性问题与核族在参数空间上的微分结构联系起来。具体方法包括：1）证明当核被非平凡微分或差分-微分算子湮灭时，必然存在无限多个不同混合度量产生相同密度；2）给出此类算子存在的可验证条件，覆盖位置-尺度高斯、位置-尺度Student-t、Gamma、Beta、Dirichlet、负二项及非中心卡方等分布族；3）对指数族适用条件进行推广；4）建立不可辨识情形下的极小极大下界；5）反向刻画三类保持可辨识性的核。

Result: 文章得到了几个关键结果：首先，建立了不可辨识性与微分算子湮灭核之间的等价刻画关系。其次，证明了参数维度超过充分统计量维度的任何指数族都满足不可辨识条件，并将此推广到分数函数多项式增长的核。第三，在不可辨识情形下给出了Wasserstein距离下估计误差的极小极大下界。最后，识别出三类保持可辨识性并支持非参数统计推断的核族。

Conclusion: 无限混合模型的可辨识性不能想当然地假定成立；在广泛常见分布族中，不可辨识性源于核函数关于参数存在微分结构的"冗余"，这本质上与指数族中参数维度超过充分统计量维度有关。作者既给出了不可辨识的充分条件，也给出了可辨识的构造性类别，为实践中的模型选择和统计推断提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study identifiability of mixing measures in infinite mixture models. We show that, in many common cases, lack of identifiability can be characterized in terms of certain differential structures of the kernel family with respect to its parameters. In our main results, we prove that when the kernel is annihilated by a non-trivial differential or difference-differential operator over the parameter space, there exist infinitely many distinct mixing measures yielding the same mixture density. We give verifiable conditions for such operators to exist, covering many common cases, including the location-scale Gaussian, location-scale Student-t, Gamma, Beta, Dirichlet, negative binomial and non-central Chi-squared families. Furthermore, our conditions apply to any exponential family whose parameter dimension exceeds the dimension of its sufficient statistic and, more generally, to kernels with polynomially-growing score functions. We complement our results with a minimax lower bound on the estimation error for the mixing measure in the Wasserstein distance under non-identifiability. On the flips side, we describe three classes of kernels for which identifiability is preserved and nonparametric statistical inference remains possible.

</details>


### [108] [Conditionally Resampled Sliding-Window Count Kernels: Spectral-Gap Bounds and Poincaré Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08678)
*Yanjin Xiang,Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限状态可逆马尔可夫链的滑动窗口计数核的谱间隙，证明了对于任意固定的严格正可逆转移核，长度为n的计数核的谱间隙下界为c(P)/n，并通过线性统计量给出了匹配的O(1/n)上界，从而得到精确的Θ_P(1/n)阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口计数过程在统计与概率应用中广泛出现，但其诱导的计数核一般不是马尔可夫的。研究其谱间隙对于理解计数统计量的收敛速度和方差上界至关重要，此前缺乏对该计数核谱间隙的系统性刻画。

Method: 证明分为两部分：(1) 利用平稳路径律的鞅振荡不等式，结合坐标振荡与计数核Dirichlet形式的直接比较，建立Poincaré不等式；(2) 通过计数向量的线性统计量构造匹配的O(1/n)上界，从而给出谱间隙的精确估计。

Result: 对任意固定的严格正可逆马尔可夫核P，得到Gap(Ṕ_n) ≥ c(P)/n的下界以及O(1/n)的匹配上界，即Gap(Ṕ_n)=Θ_P(1/n)。由此推导出计数统计量的局部到全局方差界，以及矩阵值经验平均的算子范数浓度界。

Conclusion: 该工作为滑动窗口计数核的谱理论提供了完整刻画，建立的结果可应用于有限窗口计数统计量的方差控制和矩阵浓度分析，具有理论和方法上的双重价值。

Abstract: We study the conditionally resampled sliding-window count kernel associated with the empirical counts of length-$n$ windows from a stationary finite-state reversible Markov chain. Although the resulting count process is generally not Markov, its stationary one-step conditional law defines a genuine Markov kernel. For every fixed strictly positive reversible kernel \(P\) on a finite state space, we present a Poincaré inequality for the induced count kernel $\tP_n$ of length $n$. In other words, we derive the lower bound of the spectral gap $\Gap(\tP_n)$ of $\tP_n$ as \[ \Gap(\tP_n)\ge \frac{c(P)}{n}, \] where \(c(P)>0\) depends only on \(P\). The proof combines a martingale oscillation inequality for the stationary path law with a direct comparison of coordinate oscillations to the Dirichlet form of the count kernel. A linear statistic of the count vector gives the matching \(O(1/n)\) upper bound, so for every fixed strictly positive reversible \(P\) one has \(\Gap(\tP_n)=Θ_P(1/n)\). The resulting count-space Poincaré inequality yields a local-to-global variance bound for finite-window count statistics and, together with a general matrix-concentration principle, operator-norm concentration for matrix-valued empirical averages.

</details>


### [109] [Limiting eigen-structure of spiked sample covariance matrices under missing observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09135)
*Haotian Cheng,Huiqin Li,Yanqing Yin,Zhixiang Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下高维PCA的渐近行为，基于随机矩阵理论，证明尖峰样本特征值仍具有渐近正态性，但极限参数与完整数据情形显著不同，并据此提出检验尖峰总体独立结构的测试方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维PCA是现代数据分析的重要工具，但缺失数据会扭曲PCA性能并使统计推断复杂化。已有研究大多假设数据完整，缺失机制对PCA渐近性质的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在带缺失观测的尖峰总体模型下，利用随机矩阵理论的最新进展研究PCA的渐近行为，推导样本特征值的极限分布，并在该结果基础上构建独立性检验统计量。

Result: 证明了缺失数据下尖峰样本特征值仍满足渐近正态性，但极限参数（均值与方差）与完整数据情形有本质差异，反映出缺失机制的非平凡影响。

Conclusion: 缺失数据对高维PCA的极限行为有实质性影响，所提出的检验可用以评估尖峰总体的独立结构，为实践中缺失数据下的统计推断提供理论指导。

Abstract: High-dimensional Principal Component Analysis (PCA) has become an essential tool in modern data analysis, offering dimensionality reduction and feature extraction. However, the presence of missing data introduces significant challenges, distorting the performance of PCA and complicating statistical inference. In this paper, we study the asymptotic behavior of PCA under a spiked population model with missing observations, leveraging recent advances in random matrix theory. We demonstrate that while the spiked sample eigenvalues exhibit asymptotic normality, the limiting parameters differ substantially from those in the complete data case, reflecting the non?trivial influence of the missing data mechanism. As an application of our results, we propose a test to evaluate the independent structure of a spiked population.

</details>


### [110] [Estimating conditional expectation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09561)
*Hông Vân Lê*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将条件期望估计问题视为不适定逆问题，基于Vapnik定理的推广提出求解方法，并导出了条件期望估计样本误差的新上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件期望估计在统计学习中是一个基础但具有挑战性的问题，将其视为不适定逆问题有助于从理论上更深入地理解其估计误差性质。

Method: 将条件期望估计建模为Hilbert空间中的随机不适定逆问题，推广Vapnik定理（Theorem 7.2）来求解该问题。

Result: 导出了条件期望估计样本误差的一个新的上界。

Conclusion: 通过将条件期望估计与不适定逆问题建立联系，并推广Vapnik定理，获得了更优的样本误差上界，为条件期望估计提供了新的理论保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of estimating conditional expectations as an ill-posed inverse problem. We propose a solution based on a generalization of Vapnik's theorem \cite[Theorem 7.2]{Vapnik1998} for solving stochastic ill-posed problems in Hilbert spaces. As an application, we derive a new upper bound for sample errors of conditional expectation estimation.

</details>


### [111] [Censored Heteroscedastic Extremes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09736)
*Martin Bladt,Theodor Henningsen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机右删失条件下非独立同分布极值观测的尾部异质性估计问题。提出了基于Beran型估计量的事件scedasis函数估计方法，并扩展到非独立同分布情形下的条件Nelson-Aalen和Beran理论，通过模拟和法国财险理赔数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非删失情形下，事件scedasis函数可以描述不同设计点对尾部的相对贡献，但在随机右删失条件下，观测到的尾部异质性会被删失scedasis函数污染，直接应用非删失技术会估计错误的目标对象。

Method: 提出Beran型估计量来估计相对事件scedasis函数；将生存分析方法扩展到非独立同分布情形，发展上阶统计量条件下的Nelson-Aalen和Beran理论；推导条件Nelson-Aalen估计量的鞅阵列表示，并给出仅依赖样本分数和带宽的显式误差界。

Result: 在温和条件下证明了估计量的一致性；模拟实验验证了方法的有限样本性能；法国财产责任保险理赔数据的应用表明，异质性删失会显著扭曲朴素的scedasis估计结果。

Conclusion: 本文为右删失极值数据的尾部异质性估计提供了理论保证和实用工具，填补了删失条件下scedasis估计的理论空白，对保险等领域的高尾风险管理具有实际意义。

Abstract: We study estimation of tail heterogeneity for non-identically distributed extreme observations subject to random right-censoring. In the uncensored setting, such heterogeneity is described by the event scedasis function, which measures the relative contribution of different design points to the upper tail. Under censoring, however, the observed tail heterogeneity is contaminated by the censoring scedasis functions, and applying uncensored techniques targets the wrong object. We propose a Beran-type estimator of the relative event scedasis, which is consistent under mild conditions. To obtain these results, survival analysis representations at an upper order statistics are extended to the non-identically distributed case; specifically, we develop conditional Nelson--Aalen and Beran theory on increasing intervals whose random endpoint is dominated, with probability tending to one, by a deterministic high local quantile. In particular, we derive a martingale array representation of the conditional Nelson--Aalen estimator with explicit error bounds depending only on the sample fraction and the bandwidth. Simulations demonstrate the finite-sample performance of the method, and an application to French property-casualty insurance claims illustrates how heterogeneous censoring can distort naive scedasis estimates.

</details>


### [112] [Dynamic $e$-closure for online hypotheses with any-time-valid evidence: closure principles and projective mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09927)
*Rianne de Heide*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了动态e-闭包（dynamic e-closure）理论，用于处理两个轴向上的顺序测试问题：新假设随时间到达，同时已有假设的证据可被任意停止时间检视。该理论在全局停止时间下实现了同步停止FDR控制和同步SupFDR控制，并建立了与规范化损失过程生成的动态闭包之间的完备性对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代测试问题在两个维度上呈现顺序性：新假设可能随时间不断到达，而已有假设的证据持续演变并可在任意停止时间被检验。传统的e闭包理论无法处理这类动态场景，需要一种新的理论框架来统一和控制这类跨时间分布的假设测试中的错误发现率。

Method: 作者发展了动态e-闭包框架：在全局停止时间下，活跃的真零假设交集是随机的，通过未来扩展一致性（future-extension coherence）将其证书与固定的终端交集进行比较，实现同步FDR控制。当证书具有时间单调性时，该闭包控制同步SupFDR并具有集合持久性。对于逐点合并，固定维度的可容许性与普通的任意依赖e-合并等价；跨时间水平的一致性要求单一全局可求和权重序列以及e值对一的精确中性。

Result: 证明了每个满足同步停止FDR或同步SupFDR准则的过程都被包含在由规范化损失过程生成的动态闭包中。在可数无限假设宇宙中，可容许的点值类中不存在非平凡的对称合并。理论从FDP推广到有界损失（关于可能真零配置单调且关于报告行为局部）。此外还给出了一个一致性反例、持久性构造以及全局有效的共享控制模型。

Conclusion: 动态e-闭包为两类顺序维度下的假设检验提供了统一的理论框架，建立了动态场景下FDR控制过程的完备性刻画，并将结果推广到更一般的损失函数。该框架具有广泛适用性，但也揭示了在无限假设宇宙中对称可容许合并的局限性。

Abstract: Many modern testing problems are sequential along two axes: new hypotheses may arrive over time, while evidence for hypotheses already under consideration continues to evolve and may be inspected at arbitrary stopping times. We develop dynamic $e$-closure for this setting. At a global stopping time the active true-null intersection is random. Future-extension coherence allows its certificate to be compared with that of a fixed terminal intersection, yielding simultaneous stopped-FDR control. If the certificates are also time-monotone, the resulting closure controls simultaneous SupFDR and is setwise persistent. Conversely, every procedure satisfying either criterion is contained in a dynamic closure generated by canonical normalized-loss processes. For pointwise mergers, fixed-dimensional admissibility is equivalent to ordinary arbitrary-dependence $e$-merging. Coherence across horizons then forces a single globally summable weight sequence and exact neutrality under padding by the $e$-value one; on a countably infinite hypothesis universe, this rules out nontrivial symmetric mergers in the admissible pointwise class. The theory extends from FDP to bounded losses that are monotone in the possible true-null configuration and local in the reported action. We also give a coherence counterexample, persistent constructions, and a globally valid shared-control model.

</details>
